# Experiment registry of this release

One row per answer file in this release (3041 files in runs/results/ and runs/results_oolong/, 149 data sets), generated by release/make_public_registry.py from the files themselves. Columns: length (d1 = 43k tokens, d16 = about 0.5M, d64 = about 1.9M for LongState-Saga; full = the whole record), method, reader (as in the file name: `claudethink8000` and `ollamathink` = thinking on, the Ollama tag gives size and quantization), reading budget in tokens, tag (prompt variant, ablation or builder), answers (lines in the file), judged (the released files of the free-form judges under runs/judged_*); every file listed is among the stored inputs of release/reproduce_tables.py (release/STORED_OUTPUTS_SHA256.txt). Runs that no reported number uses are not part of the release.
